## S-DU PAO-LO SIA PEH KO-LONG-TO NYING DI-IH-FONG SHÜ-SING. 使徒<u>保羅</u>寫撥<u>哥林多</u>人第一封書信

### 1K. I.

<ruby><rb>奉</rb><rt>VONG</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>召來</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所提尼</rb><rt>Sô-di-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>*mông-mông*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召來</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>；（<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt></ruby>。）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>賜落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>富足</rb><rt>fu-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>口才</rb><rt>kʽeo-dzæ</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>知識</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt></ruby>；<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>靠實了</rb><rt>kʽao-jih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>缺少</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>顯出來</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>底</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>信實個</rb><rt>sing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>相同</rb><rt>siang-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做樣</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>做樣</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>合而爲一</rb><rt>ʽeh-r-we-ih</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革來</rb><rt>Keh-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話爾拉</rb><rt>wô-ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；」「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞波羅</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；」「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>磯法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；」「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>分開了</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ-de</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保羅</u>個</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革利士布</rb><rt>Keh-li-z-pu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦猶</rb><rt>Kyüô-yiu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>；<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>嘸</rb><rt>m</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>記弗起</rb><rt>kyi-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>*tsʽa*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>落空</rb><rt>lôh-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十字架個</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>滅亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愚笨個</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>得救</rb><rt>teh-kyiu</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>廢掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>見識</rb><rt>kyin-sih</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>見識</rb><rt>kyin-sih</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>辯論</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>愚笨</rb><rt>nyü-beng</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>終於</rb><rt>*cong-ü*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>愚笨</rb><rt>nyü-beng</rt><rb>傳教個</rb><rt>djün-kyiao-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>*yi-zin*</rt><rb>隱藏的個</rb><rt>ing-dzông-tih-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>*tsʽong-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>萬代</rb><rt>væn-dæ</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>時世個</rb><rt>z-shü-go</rt><rb>官長</rb><rt>kwun-tsiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>識得個</rb><rt>sih-teh-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt><rb>釘煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>榮華個</rb><rt>yüong-wô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>*Sing-shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>備好間個</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>聽聞過</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>忖着過</rb><rt>tsʽeng-djôh-ko</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>深奧個</rb><rt>sing-ao-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>接受個</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>教個</rb><rt>kao-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>教個</rb><rt>kao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>靈勒緊個</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>靈勒緊個</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>感化</rb><rt>ken-hwô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>靈個</rb><rt>Ling-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愚笨個</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>靈勒緊個</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>查察<sup> </sup>個</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」 <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Dzô-tsʽah, *ʽôh* *wô*, bin-cʽih. 查察，或話，辨出。（原註）</sup>

### 1K. III.

<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>靈勒緊個</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>加弗起</rb><rt>kô-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>加弗起</rb><rt>kô-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>妒忌</rb><rt>tu-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分開個</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>款式</rb><rt>kwʽun-shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保羅</u>個</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞波羅</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo-go</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>\[1\] ʽÔh wô, kyih-tông. 或話，結黨。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] ʽÔh wô, tsiao *shü-kæn-zông* nying ka tso. 或話，照世間上人介做。（原註）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>亞波羅</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>種個</rb><rt>cong-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞波羅</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>澆個</rb><rt>kyiao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>種個</rb><rt>cong-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸要緊</rb><rt>m-iao-kying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>澆個</rb><rt>kyiao-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸要緊</rb><rt>m-iao-kying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大起來</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>要緊</rb><rt>*iao-kying*</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>種個</rb><rt>cong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>澆個</rb><rt>kyiao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>勞碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>幫手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>田畝</rb><rt>din-meo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好像</rb><rt>hao-ziang</rt><rb>聰明個</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>作頭</rb><rt>tsôh-deo</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>放好</rb><rt>fông-hao</rt><rb>牆腳</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>去造上去</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>起上去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-zông-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>放間個</rb><rt>fông-kæn-go</rt><rb>牆腳</rb><rt>*ziang-kyiah*</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>除此之外</rb><rt>djü-tsʽ-ts-nga</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>牆腳</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寶石</rb><rt>pao-zah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>*ʽôh-tsia*</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稻草</rb><rt>dao-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蘆竿</rb><rt>lu-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去造</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>牆腳個</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah-go</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>；<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>各人個</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>看出</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>表</rb><rt>piao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>猛</rb><rt>*mang*</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>顯出來</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>試出</rb><rt>s-cʽih</rt><rb>各人個</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>起上去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-zông-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>工鈿</rb><rt>*kong-din*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>縫裏</rb><rt>vong-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>救出個</rb><rt>*kyiu-cʽih-go*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>壞掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>壞掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>就是個</rb><rt>ziu-z-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Wæ-diao; *ʽôh* *wô*, hwe-diao. 壞掉；或話，毀掉。（原註）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Kôh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>騙</rb><rt>pʽin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聰明個</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>愚笨個</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>*jih-we*</rt><rb>聰明個</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愚笨個</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>詐計</rb><rt>tsô-kyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>*ih-kyü*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>忖頭</rb><rt>tsʽeng-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>虛妄</rb><rt>hyü-vông</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>誇口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞波羅</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>磯法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>世界</rb><rt>shü-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt></ruby>；<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. IV.

<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>手下人</rb><rt>siu-ʽô-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>賬房</rb><rt>tsiang-vông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>賬房</rb><rt>tsiang-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>*dzô-tsʽah*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>毛病</rb><rt>*mao-bing*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>未必</rb><rt>vi-pih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>囥的個</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>㪗出來</rb><rt>tʽeo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>*cong-nying*</rt><rb>心裏個</rb><rt>sing-li-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>顯出來</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>門份個</rb><rt>*meng-veng-go*</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>藉</rb><rt>tsia</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞波羅</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>印子</rb><rt>ing-ts</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>印頭</rb><rt>ing-deo</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>過如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>*Sing-shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>蕩</rb><rt>dông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>自尊自大</rb><rt>zi-tseng-zi-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>弗起</rb><rt>feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>誇口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>？

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>喫飽了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>富足了</rb><rt>fu-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>*de*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>巴弗能夠</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>*jih-dzæ*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>擺出</rb><rt>pa-cʽih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>卑賤個</rb><rt>pe-zin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>*væn-nying*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>可比</rb><rt>*kʽo-pi*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>看頭</rb><rt>kʽen-deo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愚笨</rb><rt>nyü-beng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聰明個</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>剛強個</rb><rt>kông-gyiang-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>體面</rb><rt>tʽi-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>倒霉個</rb><rt>tao-me-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>Dzih-tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>出膊</rb><rt>cʽih-poh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>捱打</rb><rt>ngæ-tang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一定個</rb><rt>ih-ding-go</rt><rb>庉處</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>謗毀</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>垃圾</rb><rt>leh-seh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>鏖糟個</rb><rt>ao-tsao-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>值鈿個</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>做說</rb><rt>tso-shih</rt><rb>提醒</rb><rt>di-sing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>*Ng-lah-go*</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>*cong-nyiang*</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自尊自大</rb><rt>zi-tseng-zi-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>惡像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯個</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>考究</rb><rt>kʽao-kyiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>自尊自大</rb><rt>zi-tseng-zi-do</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>考究</rb><rt>*kʽao-kyiu*</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>口才</rb><rt>kʽeo-dzæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>口才</rb><rt>kʽeo-dzæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>教方</rb><rt>kao-fông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>仁愛</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt><rb>溫柔個</rb><rt>w̆eng-ziu-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>誇口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>一眼個</rb><rt>ih-ngæn-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>和團</rb><rt>ʽo-dön</rt><rb>發起</rb><rt>fah-kyʽi</rt><rb>酵</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>簇新</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sing</rt><rb>一團</rb><rt>ih-dön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸酵水</rb><rt>m-kao-shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>殺掉了</rb><rt>sah-diao-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>惡意</rb><rt>ôh-i</rt><rb>惡毒個</rb><rt>ôh-doh-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>清潔</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt><rb>無酵餅</rb><rt>vu-kao-ping</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇封</rb><rt>keh-fong</rt><rb>信裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>湊隊</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>姦淫個</rb><rt>kæn-ying-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>貪財個</rb><rt>tʽen-dzæ-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>索詐個</rb><rt>sôh-tsô-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>湊隊</rb><rt>*tsʽeo-de*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>巧勒</rb><rt>kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>跳出</rb><rt>tʽiao-cʽih</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>貪</rb><rt>tʽen</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>𧮙罵個</rb><rt>zôh-mô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>喫醉個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>索詐</rb><rt>sôh-tsô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>湊隊</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>*væn*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>外頭個</rb><rt>nga-deo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>裏頭個</rb><rt>li-deo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>外頭個</rb><rt>nga-deo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」 ### 1K. VI.

<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>NG-LAH-GO</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一起</rb><rt>ih-kyʽi</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖徒個</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>倒倒</rb><rt>tao-tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>霉</rb><rt>me</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>理處</rb><rt>li-cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>中央個</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>未必</rb><rt>vi-pih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>喫食</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>壞掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>肢體</rb><rt>ts-tʽi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>肢體</rb><rt>ts-tʽi</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>婊子個</rb><rt>piao-ts-go</rt><rb>肢體</rb><rt>ts-tʽi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>斷斷</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>膠粘</rb><rt>kao-nyin</rt><rb>婊子</rb><rt>piao-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>*Sing-shü*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>膠粘</rb><rt>kao-nyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>犯個</rb><rt>væn-go</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt><rb>買來個</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩樣</rb><rt>*liang-yiang*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. VII.

<ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>KÔNG-TAO</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>Nen-nyüing</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>門份個</rb><rt>meng-veng-go</rt><rb>好意</rb><rt>hao-i</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>Lao-nyüing</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>男𡢿個</rb><rt>nen-nyüing-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>*cü-i*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>老𡢿個</rb><rt>lao-nyüing-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>*cü-i*</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>耽誤</rb><rt>tæn-ngwu</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>暫時</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>相別</rb><rt>*siang-bih*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>專門</rb><rt>cün-meng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>禱告個</rb><rt>tao-kao-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>慾心</rb><rt>yüôh-sing</rt><rb>禁弗住</rb><rt>kying-feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>引誘</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>體諒</rb><rt>tʽi-liang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>*feh-jün*</rt><rb>巴弗能夠</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>*sô*</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>*teh-djôh-go*</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>慾心</rb><rt>yüôh-sing</rt><rb>禁弗住</rb><rt>kying-feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>*neng-s*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>婚配</rb><rt>hweng-pʽe</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>動</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>慾火</rb><rt>yüôh-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>婚配</rb><rt>hweng-pʽe</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>婚配個</rb><rt>hweng-pʽe-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>*feng-fu-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>Lao-nyüing</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>和攏</rb><rt>ʽo-long</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>餘多個</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>*wô-go*</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>Tʽông-jün</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>住東</rb><rt>djü-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>住東</rb><rt>djü-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尊起了</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>尊起了</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尊起個</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>Tʽông-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>縛住了</rb><rt>bo-djü-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>和睦</rb><rt>ʽo-moh</rt><rb>做主</rb><rt>tso-cü</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>未可知</rb><rt>vi-kʽo-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>未可知</rb><rt>vi-kʽo-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, z dzæ-ü ʽo-moh. 或話，殺掉做祭物。（原註）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召來個</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>遮掩</rb><rt>tsô-in</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召來個</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸要緊</rb><rt>m-iao-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>嘸要緊</rb><rt>m-iao-kying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>要緊</rb><rt>*iao-kying*</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Kôh-nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召來</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召來</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>弗妨得個</rb><rt>feh-fông-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>趁</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>機會</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>召來個</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>釋放了</rb><rt>sih-fông-liao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召來個</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt><rb>買來個</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召來</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>KÔNG-TAO</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>哀憐</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>*ngô-go*</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>現在個</rb><rt>yin-dzæ-go</rt><rb>難頭</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>結攏</rb><rt>kyih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>拆開</rb><rt>tsʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>結攏</rb><rt>kyih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>Jün-r</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>出嫁</rb><rt>cʽih-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>箇種介個</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>肉身個</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>難處</rb><rt>næn-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>不忍相</rb><rt>peh-jing-siang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kying-kyih. 或話，緊急。（原註）</sup>

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日腳</rb><rt>Nyih-kyiah</rt><rb>短了</rb><rt>tön-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>嘸老𡢿</rb><rt>m-lao-nyüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>啼哭個</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>快活個</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>買進個</rb><rt>ma-tsing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>過於</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>擔</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>心事</rb><rt>sing-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>M-neh</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>馬籠頭</rb><rt>mô-long-deo</rt><rb>套</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>頭裏</rb><rt>deo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>*tso-nying*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>體面</rb><rt>tʽi-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>一成濟路</rb><rt>ih-dzing-tsi-lu</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>懦弱個</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>知識</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>！<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傷</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>堅固個</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>喫食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>帶累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kô-zông; *ʽôh*, to-deo. 或話，加上；或，多頭。（原註）</sup>
