### 1K. IX.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Dziu</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>*s-du*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>職份個</rb><rt>tsih-veng-go</rt><rb>印證</rb><rt>ing-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>喝個</rb><rt>hah-go</rt><rb>章程</rb><rt>công-dzing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>教會裏個</rb><rt>*kyiao-we-li-go*</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>章程</rb><rt>công-dzing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>餘多個</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>磯法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>獨獨</rb><rt>doh-doh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>章程</rb><rt>công-dzing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>當兵</rb><rt>tông-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>口糧</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>種</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>一園</rb><rt>ih-yün</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>羊奶</rb><rt>yiang-na</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>*tæn-tsih*</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>*i-s*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法裏</rb><rt>lih-fah-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>碾穀個</rb><rt>Nyin-koh-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>套</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>原是</rb><rt>Nyün-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耕種個</rb><rt>Kang-cong-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>耕種</rb><rt>kang-cong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該個</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>稻</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該個</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>扎開</rb><rt>tsah-kʽæ</rt><rb>靈勒緊</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>肉身個</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>別人</rb><rt>Bih-nying</rt><rb>尚且</rb><rt>jông-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>*do*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何況</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>擔當</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>聖殿個</rb><rt>sing-din-go</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>侍值</rb><rt>z-dzih</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>祭壇上個</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-zông-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>*li-veh*</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>度日</rb><rt>du-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>箇番</rb><rt>keh-fæn</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>誇口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>虛話</rb><rt>hyü-*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>誇口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>巧勒</rb><rt>kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>賞</rb><rt>sông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>賬房個</rb><rt>tsiang-vông-go</rt><rb>缺分</rb><rt>kyʽüih-veng</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>賞</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*Ziu-z*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>費</rb><rt>fi</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>本來</rb><rt>*peng-læ*</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人家個</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>情願</rb><rt>*dzing-nyün*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>衆人個</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打夥</rb><rt>tang-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>名下個</rb><rt>ming-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打夥</rb><rt>tang-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>名下個</rb><rt>ming-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>名下個</rb><rt>ming-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>嘸律法個</rb><rt>m-lih-fah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打夥</rb><rt>tang-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>嘸律法</rb><rt>m-lih-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>嘸律法個</rb><rt>m-lih-fah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>嘸律法</rb><rt>m-lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。）<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>懦弱個</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打夥</rb><rt>tang-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>懦弱個</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>懦弱個</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>打夥</rb><rt>tang-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>終於</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>福音個</rb><rt>foh-ing-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>校場裏</rb><rt>kao-dziang-li</rt><rb>賽</rb><rt>sæ</rt><rb>奔競</rb><rt>peng-gying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>奔</rb><rt>peng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>賞</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>奔法</rb><rt>peng-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>賽</rb><rt>sæ</rt><rb>本事</rb><rt>peng-z</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>做忌</rb><rt>tso-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>*tso-fah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一頂</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>壞掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>冠冕</rb><rt>kwun-min</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>*siang*</rt><rb>一頂</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>壞掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>奔</rb><rt>peng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>嘸數目賬</rb><rt>m-su-moh-tsiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>拳頭</rb><rt>gyün-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>空裏</rb><rt>kʽong-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>壓治</rb><rt>ah-djü</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尚恐怕</rb><rt>zông-kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>*foh-ing*</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>踢出</rb><rt>tʽih-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. X.

<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>雲個</rb><rt>yüing-go</rt><rb>下頭</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>海個</rb><rt>hæ-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>雲裏</rb><rt>yüing-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>做樣</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>靈勒緊個</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>口糧</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>做樣</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>靈勒緊個</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>靈勒緊</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>流出來個</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大半</rb><rt>ih-do-pun</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>榜樣</rb><rt>pông-yiang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>貪</rb><rt>tʽen</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>貪</rb><rt>tʽen</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>坐帶落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt><rb>喫喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>嬉戲</rb><rt>hyi-hyi</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>二萬</rb><rt>nyi-væn</rt><rb>三千</rb><rt>sæn-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>毒蛇</rb><rt>doh-dzô</rt><rb>喪命</rb><rt>sông-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>抄滅個</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>榜樣</rb><rt>pông-yiang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>警戒</rb><rt>kying-kyiæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>末代</rb><rt>meh-dæ</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>臨着個</rb><rt>ling-djôh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>未免</rb><rt>vi-min</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tso ing-tsing; *ʽôh*, tông yiang-ts. 或話，做印證；或，當樣子。（原註）</sup>

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着個</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>平常</rb><rt>bing-djông</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>信實個</rb><rt>sing-jih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>過於</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>迷惑個</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>出路</rb><rt>cʽih-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>擋勒住</rb><rt>tông-leh-djü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>男個</rb><rt>nen-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>女個</rb><rt>nyü-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>頭裏</rb><rt>deo-li</rt><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>帶累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>嘸體面</rb><rt>m-tʽi-min</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>頭裏</rb><rt>deo-li</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>帶累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>嘸體面</rb><rt>m-tʽi-min</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>*deo-fah*</rt><rb>剃掉</rb><rt>tʽi-diao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>一色斯樣個</rb><rt>ih-seh-s-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>剪掉</rb><rt>tsin-diao</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>*deo-fah*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>剪掉</rb><rt>tsin-diao</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>剃掉</rb><rt>tʽi-diao</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>*deo-fah*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, li-tsih. 或話，禮節。（原註）</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>Nen</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應當</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>男個</rb><rt>nen-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>造出來</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>造個</rb><rt>*zao-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>應當</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭裏</rb><rt>deo-li</rt><rb>帶的</rb><rt>ta-tih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>隔開</rb><rt>kah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>隔開</rb><rt>kah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>生出來</rb><rt>*sang-cʽih-læ*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>究竟</rb><rt>kyiu-kying</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *doh*, *kyiao-we-li-go* s-tsʽa. 有得讀，教會裏個使差。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Sô* *wô* gyün-ping, *z* *deo-li* *ta-tih-go* *tong-si*, *tso* *voh* *nen* *go* *kyi-ʽao*. 所話權柄，是頭裏帶的個東西，做服男個記號。（原註）</sup>

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批評相</rb><rt>pʽi-bing-siang</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遮蓋</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>*deo*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>合理</rb><rt>ʽeh-li</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>合理</rb><rt>ʽeh-li</rt></ruby>？<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>生成個</rb><rt>sang-dzing-go</rt><rb>理性</rb><rt>li-sing</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>長長</rb><rt>dziang-dziang</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘸體面</rb><rt>m-tʽi-min</rt></ruby>；<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>長長</rb><rt>dziang-dziang</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>遮蓋個</rb><rt>tsô-kæ-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tông siu-pʽao. 或話，當手帕。（原註）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>*ng*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聚攏間</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>頭一門</rb><rt>Deo-ih-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>教會裏</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾分</rb><rt>kyi-feng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>結黨</rb><rt>kyih-tông</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>試出了</rb><rt>s-cʽih-liao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>看出</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tsang-leng. 或話，爭論。（原註）</sup>

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>晚餐</rb><rt>væn-tsʽæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>搶搶</rb><rt>tsʽiang-tsʽiang</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>肚飢個</rb><rt>du-kyi-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫醉個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>看輕</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>稱讚弗來</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, feh neng-keo. 有得翻，弗能夠。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, gyüong-kwʽu-go. 或話，窮苦個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>傳授</rb><rt>djün-ziu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>祝謝仔</rb><rt>coh-zia-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼開</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>𤖼開個</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>*cong*-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天使個</rb><rt>tʽin-s-go</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>*yiang-yiang*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>仁愛</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>響個</rb><rt>hyiang-go</rt><rb>銅器</rb><rt>dong-*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>寋個</rb><rt>gæ-go</rt><rb>大鈸</rb><rt>do-beh</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>周濟</rb><rt>tsiu-tsi</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>*gyüong-nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>捨出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦煞</rb><rt>me-sah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>仁愛</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>無益</rb><rt>vu-ih</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. XIV.

<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>NG-LAH</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>上緊</rb><rt>zông-kying</rt><rb>追着</rb><rt>tse-djôh</rt><rb>仁愛</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling-go</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>要緊</rb><rt>iao-kying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>德氣</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勸化</rb><rt>kyʽün-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>安慰個</rb><rt>en-w̆e-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>Kông</rt><rb>別處</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>德氣</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>德氣</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>別處</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>別處</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>翻譯</rb><rt>fæn-yih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>德氣</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假如</rb><rt>kyüô-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>別處</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>知識</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>於</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>？<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>嘸靈氣</rb><rt>m-ling-kyʽi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>笛子</rb><rt>dih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>琵琶</rb><rt>bi-bô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>發出個</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>分別</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>吹出</rb><rt>cʽü-cʽih</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>腔</rb><rt>*kyʽiang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>彈出</rb><rt>dæn-cʽih</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>調</rb><rt>*diao*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>號頭</rb><rt>ʽao-deo</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>清通個</rb><rt>tsʽing-tʽong-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>Feh-jün</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>靈性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>祝讚</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白個</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>懂</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祝謝好</rb><rt>coh-zia-hao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>17</sup> <sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>隱藏的個</rb><rt>ing-dzông-tih-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>*i-s*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>顯</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>*ʽôh-tsia*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一首</rb><rt>ih-siu</rt><rb>詩</rb><rt>s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>別處</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>翻譯</rb><rt>fæn-yih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>德氣</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>別處</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只消</rb><rt>tsih-siao</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>多則</rb><rt>to-tseh</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>翻譯</rb><rt>fæn-yih</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>翻譯</rb><rt>fæn-yih</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>教會裏</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>兩三個</rb><rt>liang-sæn-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>辨別</rb><rt>bin-pih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*gyi-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>*bông-pin*</rt><rb>坐東個</rb><rt>zo-tong-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>*kông-go*</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>歇口</rb><rt>hyih-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>學</rb><rt>ʽôh</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>亂七八糟</rb><rt>lön-tsʽih-pah-tsao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>和睦</rb><rt>ʽo-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖徒個</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tsʽao-lön. 或話，吵亂。（原註）</sup>

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>教會裏</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>*nen*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>女𡢿</rb><rt>nyü-nyüing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>教會裏</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惶恐相</rb><rt>wông-kʽong-siang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>單</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靈勒緊個</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>識得個</rb><rt>sih-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>別處</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>Yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>體面相</rb><rt>tʽi-min-siang</rt><rb>挨次序</rb><rt>a-tsʽ-jü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. XV.

<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立勒住</rb><rt>lih-leh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；）<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>提醒</rb><rt>di-sing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>*foh-ing*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>空頭</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, kyi-teh. 有得翻，記得。（原註）</sup>

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>傳授</rb><rt>djün-ziu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>接受個</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>葬好仔</rb><rt>tsông-hao-ts</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>；<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>五百多</rb><rt>ng-pah-to</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>睏熟了</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大半</rb><rt>ih-do-pun</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>*yin-cʽih*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>月份</rb><rt>yüih-veng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>足</rb><rt>tsoh</rt><rb>生出個</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小個</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Dziu</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>講來</rb><rt>kông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>鬥過</rb><rt>teo-ko</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>落得</rb><rt>lôh-teh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>落得</rb><rt>lôh-teh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>騙</rb><rt>pʽin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。「<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>Tseo</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>隊</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>帶掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>死個</rb><rt>Si-go</rt><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>多謝</rb><rt>to-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>贏面</rb><rt>ying-min</rt></ruby>。

<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>親愛個</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>立定</rb><rt>lih-ding</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>廣行</rb><rt>kwông-ʽang</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>勞碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落空個</rb><rt>lôh-kʽong-go</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. XVI.

<ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>KÔNG-TAO</rt><rb>周濟</rb><rt>tsiu-tsi</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>捐項</rb><rt>kyün-ʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加拉太</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah-tʽa-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>Me</rt><rb>七日個</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>頭一日</rb><rt>deo-ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>朵化</rb><rt>tô-hwô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>進業</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抽點出</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tin-cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>積聚間</rb><rt>tsih-jü-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>纔方</rb><rt>dzæ-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>捐</rb><rt>kyün</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>舉薦個</rb><rt>kyü-tsin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>捐項</rb><rt>kyün-ʽông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>卻勒</rb><rt>kyʽiah-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, eng-we: *kʽen* 2K. 8. 6. 本文字眼，恩惠。看 2K. 8. 6.（原註）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>經過了</rb><rt>kying-ko-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬其頓</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>；（<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬其頓</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>經過個</rb><rt>kying-ko-go</rt></ruby>。）<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>耽擱記</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>冬</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>未可知</rb><rt>vi-kʽo-cü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>相見</rb><rt>siang-kyin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一落</rb><rt>ih-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>五旬節</rb><rt>Wu-shing-tsih</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一道</rb><rt>ih-dao</rt><rb>廣闊</rb><rt>kwông-kwʽeh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>輕</rb><rt>kyʽing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>安安耽耽</rb><rt>en-en-tæn-tæn</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞波羅</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>再三</rb><rt>tsæ-sæn</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*gyi-go*</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>決</rb><rt>kyüih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>便個</rb><rt>bin-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大丈夫</rb><rt>da-dziang-fu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>剛強膽氣</rb><rt>kông-gyiang-tæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>仁愛</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt><rb>做主</rb><rt>tso-cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞該亞</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô-go</rt><rb>嘗新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>單</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby> <sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>勞碌個</rb><rt>lao-loh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>福徒納</rb><rt>Foh-du-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞該古</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-kwu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>弗到之處</rb><rt>feh-tao-ts-cʽü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>補足個</rb><rt>pu-tsoh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>*sing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>安慰過了</rb><rt>en-w̆e-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>認着</rb><rt>nying-djôh</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb><u>亞西亞</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞居</rb><rt>Üô-kyü</rt><rb>拉</rb><rt>lah</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>百基拉</rb><rt>Pah-kyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>切身</rb><rt>tsʽih-sing</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>致致誠誠</rb><rt>ts-ts-dzing-dzing</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>嗅嘴個</rb><rt>hyüong-cü-go</rt><rb>禮</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>親筆</rb><rt>tsʽing-pih</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>兩句</rb><rt>*liang-kyü*</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>滅亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>愛惜心</rb><rt>æ-sih-sing</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
